Commentary 2026-05-05T14:43:07.643Z

搜索结果里没有明显的 add_on_set_parameters_callback，这意味着当前项目的 TEB 很可能只在启动/configure 时读取参数，运行中 ros2 param set 不一定会更新内部 cfg_。我再精确看 TEB 的 configure 和参数读取代码，确认能不能直接用现成面板。

TebLocalPlannerROS::TebLocalPlannerROS() 
    : costmap_ros_(nullptr), tf_(nullptr), cfg_(new TebConfig()), costmap_model_(nullptr), intra_proc_node_(nullptr),
                                           
                                           custom_via_points_active_(false), no_infeasible_plans_(0),
                                           last_preferred_rotdir_(RotType::none), initialized_(false)
{
}


TebLocalPlannerROS::~TebLocalPlannerROS()
{
}

void TebLocalPlannerROS::initialize(nav2_util::LifecycleNode::SharedPtr node)
{
  // check if the plugin is already initialized
  if(!initialized_)
  {	
    // declare parameters (ros2-dashing)
    intra_proc_node_.reset( 
            new rclcpp::Node("teb_local_planner", node->get_namespace(), 
              rclcpp::NodeOptions()));
    cfg_->declareParameters(node, name_);

    // get parameters of TebConfig via the nodehandle and override the default config
    cfg_->loadRosParamFromNodeHandle(node, name_);
    
    // reserve some memory for obstacles
    obstacles_.reserve(500);
        
    // create the planner instance
    if (cfg_->hcp.enable_homotopy_class_planning)
    {
      planner_ = PlannerInterfacePtr(new HomotopyClassPlanner(node, *cfg_.get(), &obstacles_, visualization_, &via_points_));
      RCLCPP_INFO(logger_, "Parallel planning in distinctive topologies enabled.");
    }
    else
    {
      planner_ = PlannerInterfacePtr(new TebOptimalPlanner(node, *cfg_.get(), &obstacles_, visualization_, &via_points_));
      RCLCPP_INFO(logger_, "Parallel planning in distinctive topologies disabled.");
    }
    
    // init other variables
    costmap_ = costmap_ros_->getCostmap(); // locking should be done in MoveBase.
    
    costmap_model_ = std::make_shared<dwb_critics::ObstacleFootprintCritic>();
    std::string costmap_model_name("costmap_model");
    costmap_model_->initialize(node, costmap_model_name, name_, costmap_ros_);

    cfg_->map_frame = costmap_ros_->getGlobalFrameID(); // TODO

   
    
    // Get footprint of the robot and minimum and maximum distance from the center of the robot to its footprint vertices.
    footprint_spec_ = costmap_ros_->getRobotFootprint();
    nav2_costmap_2d::calculateMinAndMaxDistances(
    footprint_spec_, robot_inscribed_radius_, robot_circumscribed_radius);

    // Add callback for dynamic parameters
    dyn_params_handler = node->add_on_set_parameters_callback(
      std::bind(&TebConfig::dynamicParametersCallback, std::ref(cfg_), std::placeholders::_1));

    // validate optimization footprint and costmap footprint
    validateFootprints(cfg_->robot_model->getInscribedRadius(), robot_inscribed_radius_, cfg_->obstacles.min_obstacle_dist);
        
    

    // setup callback for custom via-points
    via_points_sub_ = node->create_subscription<nav_msgs::msg::Path>(
                "via_points", 
                rclcpp::SystemDefaultsQoS(),
                std::bind(&TebLocalPlannerROS::customViaPointsCB, this, std::placeholders::_1));
    
    // initialize failure detector
    //rclcpp::Node::SharedPtr nh_move_base("~");
    double controller_frequency = 5;
    node->get_parameter("controller_frequency", controller_frequency);
    failure_detector_.setBufferLength(std::round(cfg_->recovery.oscillation_filter_duration*controller_frequency));
    
    // set initialized flag
    initialized_ = true;

    // This should be called since to prevent different time sources exception
    time_last_infeasible_plan_ = clock_->now();
    time_last_oscillation_ = clock_->now();
    RCLCPP_DEBUG(logger_, "teb_local_planner plugin initialized.");
  }
  else
  {
    RCLCPP_INFO(logger_, "teb_local_planner has already been initialized, doing nothing.");
  }
}

void TebLocalPlannerROS::configure(
    const rclcpp_lifecycle::LifecycleNode::WeakPtr & parent,
    std::string name,
    std::shared_ptr<tf2_ros::Buffer> tf,
    std::shared_ptr<nav2_costmap_2d::Costmap2DROS> costmap_ros) {
  nh_ = parent;

  auto node = nh_.lock();
  logger_ = node->get_logger();
  clock_ = node->get_clock();

  costmap_ros_ = costmap_ros;
  tf_ = tf;
  name_ = name;

  initialize(node);
  visualization_ = std::make_shared<TebVisualization>(node, *cfg_);
  visualization_->on_configure();

Commentary 2026-05-05T14:43:24.556Z

我确认了项目里的 TEB 代码：它确实注册了 ROS2 参数动态回调，所以 ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.xxx ... 这类修改可以运行时生效。问题是标准 RViz2 的 Navigation2 面板本身不提供 TEB 参数滑条；要“在 RViz2 里调”，需要额外做一个 RViz2 Panel 插件，或者先用 rqt/命令行做动态调参。

        RCLCPP_INFO(logger_, "Footprint model 'polygon' reloaded for trajectory optimization.");

{

  if (robot.max_vel_x_backwards <= 0)
    RCLCPP_WARN(logger_, "TebLocalPlannerROS() Param Warning: Do not choose max_vel_x_backwards to be <=0. Disable backwards driving by increasing the optimization weight for penalyzing backwards driving.");
  
  // bounds smaller than penalty epsilon
  if (robot.max_vel_x <= optim.penalty_epsilon)

Assistant 2026-05-05T14:44:56.495Z

可以，但要先分清楚：

RViz2 自带的 Navigation2 面板只能发目标点、取消导航、看状态，不能直接调 TEB 参数。
你现在这个项目里的 TEB 支持 ROS2 动态参数回调，所以可以运行时改参数，但要用：

  1. 先用 ros2 param set 或 rqt 动态调。
  2. 如果你坚持“在 RViz2 里用面板滑条调”，需要写一个 RViz2 Panel 插件。

最快可用方案 先不要写面板，直接开一个终端动态调，参数会在线生效：

ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_x 2.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_theta 1.1
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.acc_lim_theta 1.1
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_forward_drive 60.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_turning_radius 80.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_viapoint 8.0

速度平滑器也要同步：

ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_velocity "[2.0, 0.0, 1.1]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_accel "[2.0, 0.0, 1.1]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_decel "[-2.0, 0.0, -1.1]"

查看当前值：

ros2 param get /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_theta
ros2 param get /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_forward_drive
ros2 param get /velocity_smoother max_velocity

推荐调参顺序 每次只改一两项：

# 车头跟不上、歪着走
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_theta 1.1
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.acc_lim_theta 1.1

# 又开始自转/晃
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_theta 0.9
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.acc_lim_theta 0.9

# 横着切弯
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_forward_drive 80.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_turning_radius 100.0

# 路径贴合不够
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_viapoint 10.0

真正的 RViz2 面板方案 需要写一个 RViz2 Panel 插件，面板里放滑条，然后内部调用 ROS2 parameter client 去 set：

  • 目标节点：/controller_server
  • 参数名：FollowPath.max_vel_x、FollowPath.max_vel_theta 等
  • 目标节点：/velocity_smoother
  • 参数名：max_velocity、max_accel 等

面板做好后，在 RViz2 里：

Panels -> Add New Panel -> 选择你的 TEB 参数面板

然后保存 RViz 配置。

我建议下一步直接给你在项目里加一个简单 RViz2 TEB 调参面板，先放这些滑条：

max_vel_x
max_vel_theta
acc_lim_x
acc_lim_theta
min_turning_radius
weight_kinematics_forward_drive
weight_kinematics_turning_radius
weight_viapoint

这样你就能边跑仿真边拖滑条。需要改 C++/CMake/package.xml，改完要重新 colcon build。

可以。现在仿真调试目标不是“随便跑通”，而是要产出 PR 能接受的证明材料。

建议你按这个流程来：

1. 启动仿真并保存日志

cd ~/venom_ws/src/venom_vnv/simulation/venom_nav_simulation
source /opt/ros/humble/setup.bash
source ~/venom_ws/install/setup.bash
source install/setup.bash

ros2 launch rm_nav_bringup bringup_sim.launch.py \
  world:=RMUL \
  mode:=nav \
  lio:=pointlio \
  localization:=slam_toolbox \
  lio_rviz:=False \
  nav_rviz:=True

启动后重点确认日志里有：

Created controller : FollowPath of type teb_local_planner::TebLocalPlannerROS
Managed nodes are active

2. 用 RViz 发多个目标点

至少测试这些场景：

  • 直线目标：看速度是否正常；
  • 90 度转弯：看是否自转/横着走；
  • 窄通道：看是否蹭墙/侧翻；
  • 连续多个目标：看是否稳定；
  • 最终目标附近：看是否震荡或绕圈。

你要录屏，放到：

~/venom_ws/simulation_videos/

例如：

simulation_videos/teb_sim_rmul_success_1.webm

3. 动态调参

你现在优先调这几组：

车头跟不上、歪着走：

ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_theta 1.1
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.acc_lim_theta 1.1
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_velocity "[2.2, 0.0, 1.1]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_accel "[2.2, 0.0, 1.1]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_decel "[-2.2, 0.0, -1.1]"

又开始自转：

ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_theta 0.9
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.acc_lim_theta 0.9

横着切弯：

ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_forward_drive 80.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_kinematics_turning_radius 100.0
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.weight_viapoint 8.0

速度太慢：

ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.max_vel_x 2.4
ros2 param set /controller_server FollowPath.acc_lim_x 2.4
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_velocity "[2.4, 0.0, 1.0]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_accel "[2.4, 0.0, 1.0]"
ros2 param set /velocity_smoother max_decel "[-2.4, 0.0, -1.0]"

4. 每次只改 1-2 个参数

不要一次改一大堆。每次改完立刻跑同一个目标点，这样你才知道哪个参数有效。

5. 调好后写回 YAML

动态调参只对当前运行生效，重启会丢。最终稳定后，把参数写回：

src/rm_nav_bringup/config/simulation/nav2_params_sim.yaml

同时确认 velocity_smoother 和 TEB 速度限制一致。

6. 最后 PR 证明材料需要这些

你最终要准备：

 - Analyzed `/home/william/venom_ws/simulation_videos/3.webm`:
   - Navigation completed with `Recoveries: 0`, but the chassis still showed heading lag and occasional diagonal/sideways-looking motion, especially after turns and on diagonal connection segments.
   - Identified the likely cause as a mismatch between the increased forward speed (`max_vel_x: 2.2`) and the still-conservative angular limits (`max_vel_theta: 0.8`, `acc_lim_theta: 0.8`).
   - Increased steering response to `max_vel_theta: 1.0` and `acc_lim_theta: 1.0`.
   - Enabled `use_proportional_saturation: True` so TEB scales linear speed down when angular demand is saturated.
   - Relaxed angular constraint weights to `weight_max_vel_theta: 3.0` and `weight_acc_lim_theta: 2.0`.
